# AAB Current Technical Contract Catalogue

**Evidence source:** `public_html (54)(1).zip`  
**Bundle file dates:** principally 2 September 2026  
**Catalogue prepared:** 20 September 2026  
**Scope:** exact identifiers and shapes statically evidenced in the supplied website bundle.

## Controlling finding

This bundle is not a complete physical database schema export. It contains a real PHP/PostgreSQL application gateway and a large browser-side contract catalogue, but no SQL migration directory or authoritative PostgreSQL catalogue dump. Therefore this report can state exact HTTP fields, PHP response envelopes, called RPC names, UI-consumed record fields, browser contract functions and plan-only table shapes. It cannot prove the SQL types, nullability, defaults, constraints, RLS policies or deployed definitions inside PostgreSQL.

A contract is classified here as:

- **Operational gateway evidence:** the PHP code authenticates a user, resolves an actor and calls PostgreSQL.
- **Client-consumption evidence:** an HTML/JS surface sends or reads the named field.
- **Browser contract:** a JavaScript contract exposes a namespace/function or validation shape.
- **Plan-only schema:** the source itself says `PLAN_ONLY`, `PLANNED`, no execution or no table creation.
- **Retired:** the gateway returns HTTP 410 and does not fall back to the legacy persistence system.

| PostgreSQL relations/views referenced | 86 |
| Browser JavaScript contract files | 683 |
| Exported browser namespaces detected | 792 |

## Common HTTP contract

```ts
type AabSuccess<T extends object = Record<string, unknown>> = {
  ok: true;
  source?: string;
  action?: string;
  actor?: Record<string, unknown>;
} & T;

type AabFailure = {
  ok: false;
  error: string;
  detail?: string | null;
  source?: string;
  fail_closed?: true;
  action?: string;
};
```

Actions are selected by `action` in the query string, form body or JSON body. Protected action groups call the existing Supabase session bridge, extract authenticated `user.email` and `user.role`, then resolve a PostgreSQL actor through `agriculture.api_resolve_authenticated_actor(email, role, display_name)`. Governed mutations call database functions rather than issuing direct table writes, except for the explicitly visible ingredient country-workspace stamping update.

### Common failure behaviour

| Condition | Evidence response | HTTP |
|---|---|---:|
| Missing authenticated email | `authenticated_email_missing` | 401 |
| Unsupported method on protected mutation | `method_not_allowed`, `allowed: POST` | 405 |
| Capability/authority denial | group-specific error, normally `fail_closed: true` | 403 |
| Retired Airtable or SQLite action | `LEGACY_*_ACTION_RETIRED` | 410 |
| Gateway/database failure | group-specific failure with `fail_closed: true` | usually 503 |
| Unhandled PHP warning/exception | JSON `php_error`, `php_exception` or `fatal_error` | 500 |

## Exact HTTP action field catalogue

Fields separated with `|` are accepted aliases. A dash means the action has no action-specific input field visible in its literal branch; authenticated or group-level context may still be required. The **literal branch keys** column is conservative static evidence and includes both response-envelope keys and internal named SQL-bind keys; it must not be treated as a formal response schema. Database return payloads represented as `record`, `result`, `workspace`, `snapshot`, `context`, `records` or similar remain opaque JSON/rows unless a client surface demonstrates their child fields.

## Important nested data shapes

### Capability-purpose shape

```ts
interface AiPurposeSchema {
  DOMAIN: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  LAYER: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  TYPE: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  ROLE: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  INPUT: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  OUTPUT: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  DEPENDENCIES: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  DEPENDENCY_TYPE: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  REASON: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
  LIFECYCLE: unknown; // exact value type varies by contract; name is canonical
}
```

Only 15 JavaScript files in this bundle contain a literal `AI_PURPOSE_SCHEMA`. The canonical field names are evidenced, but the bundle does not establish one enforced TypeScript/JSON Schema value type across every contract.

### Reasoning signal packet

```ts
interface ReasoningSignalPacket {
  sourceReasoningEngine: string;
  targetDomainBrainId: string;
  signalType: 'MECHANISM' | 'COMPATIBILITY' | 'NOVELTY' | 'EVIDENCE' | 'CONFIDENCE' | 'LEARNING';
  signalSummary: string;
  confidence: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH' | 'INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE';
  evidenceState: 'SUPPORTED' | 'PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED' | 'CONFLICTING' | 'INSUFFICIENT' | 'UNKNOWN';
  learningImplication: string;
}
```

Blocked operational keys include `write`, `mutate`, `execute`, `deploy`, `approve`, `activate`, `delete`, `grantAuthority`, `overrideGovernance`, `changeDose`, `changeFormulation`, `certify` and `registerClaim`.

### Institution unit

```ts
interface CreateInstitutionUnitRequest {
  organization_id: string;
  unit_code: string;
  unit_name: string;
  unit_symbol: string;
  quantity_kind: string;
  canonical_unit: string;
  conversion_multiplier?: number | null;
  conversion_offset?: number; // gateway default 0
  definition_summary?: string;
  reference?: string;
}
```

### Institution metric

```ts
interface CreateInstitutionMetricRequest {
  organization_id: string;
  metric_code: string;
  metric_name: string;
  description?: string;
  value_type?: 'NUMERIC' | 'TEXT' | 'BOOLEAN' | 'JSON';
  source_unit_code?: string;
  global_metric_definition_id?: string | null;
  method_reference?: string;
}
```

### Observation capture

```ts
interface CaptureObservationRequest {
  trial_id: string;
  plot_id: string;
  template_version_id: string;
  observation_code: string;
  values: Record<string, unknown>;
  observed_at?: string | null;
  notes?: string | null;
}
```

The contract says AAB owns metric units and observers submit values, not ad-hoc units.

### Problem and transformation shapes

```ts
interface ProblemSignalRequest {
  problem_statement: string;
  problem_type?: string;       // default LOCAL_PROBLEM
  domain_code?: string;        // default AGRICULTURE
  country_workspace_id?: string | null;
  origin_type?: string;        // default HUMAN
  origin_reference?: Record<string, unknown>;
  local_context?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

interface TransformationOpportunityRequest {
  problem_signal_id: string;
  opportunity_summary: string;
  opportunity_type?: string;   // default OTHER
  scientific_resource_hypothesis?: string | null;
  novelty_mode?: string;       // default UNASSESSED
  environmental_value?: number;
  economic_value?: number;
  scientific_value?: number;   // default 0.5
  feasibility?: number;        // default 0.5
}
```

### Proposed ingredient candidate

```ts
interface ProposedIngredientCandidateRequest {
  transformation_opportunity_id: string;
  candidate_name: string;
  candidate_concept: string;
  novelty_mode?: string; // default NOVEL_INGREDIENT
  composition_plan?: unknown[];
  process_plan?: unknown[];
  target_mechanisms?: unknown[];
  predicted_functions?: unknown[];
}
```

### Intelligence activity event

```ts
interface IntelligenceActivityEventInput {
  domain?: string;              // default AGRICULTURE
  scope?: string;               // default SYSTEM
  code?: string | null;
  label?: string | null;
  type?: string;                // default COMPLETED
  headline?: string;
  trigger_type?: string | null;
  trigger_id?: string | null;
  trigger_label?: string | null;
  detail?: Record<string, unknown>;
  country?: string | null;
  correlation?: string | null;
  parent?: string | null;
}
```

## UI-consumed response shapes

The following fields are concrete because current HTML surfaces read them.

### Country collaboration snapshot

```ts
interface CountryCollaborationSnapshot {
  organizations: Array<{
    organization_id: string;
    organization_name: string;
    organization_type: string;
    organization_code: string;
    active: boolean;
  }>;
  organization_workspaces: Array<{
    workspace_name: string;
    workspace_type: string;
    default_sharing_classification: string;
    lifecycle_status: string;
  }>;
  sharing_classes: Array<{
    display_name: string;
    description: string;
    sharing_classification_code: string;
  }>;
  my_organization_memberships?: Array<{ organization_id: string }>;
}
```

### Country administration snapshot

```ts
interface CountryAdminSnapshot {
  workspace?: { country_name?: string };
  team: Array<{ display_name: string; organization_name?: string; membership_role: string; membership_status: string }>;
  objectives: Array<{ objective_title: string; objective_description: string; priority: string }>;
  regulatory_gate?: { gate_status?: string; reauthentication_required?: boolean; mfa_required?: boolean; second_reviewer_required?: boolean }>;
  backup_health?: { continuity_status?: string; last_internal_checkpoint_status?: string; external_backup_status?: string }>;
  impact?: Array<{ metric_name: string; evidence_summary: string; numeric_value?: number; text_value?: string; unit?: string }>;
}
```

## Retired interface surface

The following actions are declared retired and receive HTTP 410. Their names must not be interpreted as active capability:

- `AAB_LEGACY_AIRTABLE_ACTIONS`: `auth_probe`, `complete_trial`, `create_observation`, `create_observation_template`, `create_trial`, `create_vil`, `derive_version`, `diag`, `diag_sources`, `field_choices`, `generation_zero_retirement_delete_action`, `generation_zero_retirement_residual_scan`, `get_discovery_candidate`, `get_hypothesis`, `get_ingredient_library_record`, `get_metrics_by_ids`, `get_observation_template`, `get_trial_plots`, `get_version`, `list_crops_varieties`, `list_discovery_candidates`, `list_farm_locations`, `list_hypothesis_queue`, `list_ingredient_library`, `list_ingredients`, `list_observation_templates`, `list_observations`, `list_plots`, `list_trials`, `list_versions_debug`, `list_vil`, `resolve_context_labels`, `save_outcome_v1`, `save_vil_bulk`, `schema_probe`, `search_metrics_catalog`, `update_vil`
- `AAB_LEGACY_SQLITE_ACTIONS`: `account_activate`, `activate_invited_account`, `append_decision`, `change_password`, `community_register`, `country_issue_activation`, `country_issue_invitation`, `create_observation_v2`, `create_plot`, `export_trial_report_pdf`, `forgot_password`, `list_round_events`, `log_round_event`, `regulatory_context`, `regulatory_reauth`, `reset_password`, `set_plot_template`, `set_trial_template`, `system_health`, `today_dashboard`, `update_observation_template`

## What this bundle still cannot supply

The following require the repository migration history or an authenticated schema-only PostgreSQL export:

- authoritative deployed table and column list
- PostgreSQL data types and domains
- nullability and default expressions
- primary, foreign and unique constraints
- indexes
- triggers
- function parameter and return declarations as defined in PostgreSQL
- RLS enablement and policy expressions
- schema/function grants
- differences between the 2 September bundle and PR #16 at `6567325a30d1383fb0541691b738f1a520d038d6`

## Current-state conclusion

This ZIP proves considerably more technical wiring than the earlier logical diagram showed: authenticated HTTP gateways, PostgreSQL actor resolution, country/institution operations, formulation/trial/observation lifecycles, learning review, cognitive-state calls, resource discovery and community observation paths all have exact action names and input envelopes in the bundle. It also contains a much larger set of scientific browser contracts.

However, the report must not collapse design into reality. Many scientific schema files explicitly describe plan-only tables, read-only contracts or non-operational envelopes. The ZIP also lacks the SQL migration source and deployed catalogue required to certify the physical database schema. Consequently, this catalogue is an accurate interface-and-contract extraction from the supplied deployment bundle, not a complete certification of AAB's database implementation or its later CAP-34 state.
